RUS_tesla_minesweeper_refresh_gui = {
	set_variable_to_random = global.RUS_tesla_minesweeper_update
}

RUS_tesla_minesweeper_open = {
	set_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_window_open
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_initialised } RUS_tesla_minesweeper_repair_counts = yes }
	RUS_tesla_minesweeper_refresh_gui = yes
}

RUS_tesla_minesweeper_close = {
	clr_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_window_open
	RUS_tesla_minesweeper_refresh_gui = yes
}

				OR = { has_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_mine_63 has_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_revealed_63 }
			}
		}
		RUS_tesla_minesweeper_finish_victory = yes
	}
}

RUS_tesla_minesweeper_finish_victory = {
	clr_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_active
	set_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_won
	set_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_cooldown
	add_to_variable = { RUS_tesla_minesweeper_win_streak = 1 }
	set_variable = { RUS_tesla_minesweeper_reward_grids = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { RUS_tesla_minesweeper_rewards_remaining = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_win_streak }
	country_event = { id = RUS_tesla_minesweeper.1 days = 60 }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { RUS_tesla_minesweeper_rewards_remaining > 0 }
			any_owned_state = {
				is_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				impassable = no
				energy_infrastructure < 1
				industrial_infrastructure < 1
			}
		}
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				is_core_of = ROOT
				impassable = no
				energy_infrastructure < 1
				industrial_infrastructure < 1
			}
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = { type = energy_infrastructure level = 1 instant_build = yes }
		}
		add_to_variable = { RUS_tesla_minesweeper_reward_grids = 1 }
		add_to_temp_variable = { RUS_tesla_minesweeper_rewards_remaining = -1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { RUS_tesla_minesweeper_reward_grids > 0 } }
		RUS_tesla_refresh_power_grid_bonuses = yes
	}
	else = {
		set_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_no_reward
	}
	RUS_tesla_minesweeper_refresh_gui = yes
}

RUS_tesla_minesweeper_finish_defeat = {
	clr_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_active
	set_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_lost
	set_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_cooldown
	set_variable = { RUS_tesla_minesweeper_win_streak = 0 }
	set_variable = { RUS_tesla_minesweeper_reward_grids = 0 }
	country_event = { id = RUS_tesla_minesweeper.1 days = 60 }
	RUS_tesla_minesweeper_refresh_gui = yes
}

RUS_tesla_minesweeper_reveal_00 = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_active NOT = { has_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_marked_00 } }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_initialised } }
			set_variable = { RUS_tesla_minesweeper_first_cell = 0 }
			RUS_tesla_minesweeper_generate_board = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_mine_00 }
			RUS_tesla_minesweeper_finish_defeat = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_revealed_00
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_count_00_0 }
				RUS_tesla_minesweeper_expand_empty_cells = yes
			}
			RUS_tesla_minesweeper_check_victory = yes
		}
	}
	RUS_tesla_minesweeper_refresh_gui = yes
}

RUS_tesla_minesweeper_mark_00 = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_active NOT = { has_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_revealed_00 } }
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_marked_00 }
			clr_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_marked_00
			add_to_variable = { RUS_tesla_minesweeper_flags = -1 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { RUS_tesla_minesweeper_flags < 10 } }
			set_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_marked_00
			add_to_variable = { RUS_tesla_minesweeper_flags = 1 }
		}
	}
	RUS_tesla_minesweeper_refresh_gui = yes

		limit = { has_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_active NOT = { has_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_marked_48 } }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_initialised } }
			set_variable = { RUS_tesla_minesweeper_first_cell = 48 }
			RUS_tesla_minesweeper_generate_board = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_mine_48 }
			RUS_tesla_minesweeper_finish_defeat = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_revealed_48
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_count_48_0 }
				RUS_tesla_minesweeper_expand_empty_cells = yes
			}
			RUS_tesla_minesweeper_check_victory = yes
		}
	}
	RUS_tesla_minesweeper_refresh_gui = yes
}

RUS_tesla_minesweeper_mark_48 = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_active NOT = { has_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_revealed_48 } }
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_marked_48 }
			clr_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_marked_48
			add_to_variable = { RUS_tesla_minesweeper_flags = -1 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { RUS_tesla_minesweeper_flags < 10 } }
			set_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_marked_48
			add_to_variable = { RUS_tesla_minesweeper_flags = 1 }
		}
	}
	RUS_tesla_minesweeper_refresh_gui = yes
}

				RUS_tesla_minesweeper_expand_empty_cells = yes
			}
			RUS_tesla_minesweeper_check_victory = yes
		}
	}
	RUS_tesla_minesweeper_refresh_gui = yes
}

RUS_tesla_minesweeper_mark_60 = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_active NOT = { has_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_revealed_60 } }
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_marked_60 }
			clr_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_marked_60
			add_to_variable = { RUS_tesla_minesweeper_flags = -1 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { RUS_tesla_minesweeper_flags < 10 } }
			set_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_marked_60
			add_to_variable = { RUS_tesla_minesweeper_flags = 1 }
		}
	}
	RUS_tesla_minesweeper_refresh_gui = yes
}
